## Rights Policies

| Id | Export | Correction | Restriction | Erasure |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| adapter-provenance | include-subject-linked-provider-provenance-or-tenant-export | supersede-through-adapter-resolution-workflow | retain-minimum-reconciliation-receipt | remove-direct-link-or-pseudonymize-approved-receipt |
| guest-reservation-data | include-in-authorized-guest-or-tenant-export | replace-through-reservation-workflow | suppress-non-required-operational-use | unlink-or-anonymize-subject-identifiers |
| guest-reservation-link | include-in-authorized-guest-or-tenant-export | correct-through-reservation-guest-link-workflow | suppress-non-required-operational-use | unlink-subject-and-retain-minimum-operational-receipt |
| reservation-anonymisation-accountability | include-minimum-anonymisation-proof-in-authorized-case-ledger-or-tenant-export | append-superseding-owner-proof | retain-minimum-approved-owner-proof | retain-non-personal-minimum-owner-proof |
| reservation-anonymisation-eligibility-control | include-digests-and-blocker-in-authorized-case-ledger | re-evaluate-from-current-owner-projections | fail-closed-on-missing-stale-or-unsupported-evidence | authorize-only-the-exact-approved-current-record-version |
| reservation-data-hold-accountability | include-minimum-hold-state-in-authorized-case-ledger-or-tenant-export | immutable-append-superseding-receipt | block-anonymisation-while-any-hold-is-active | retain-until-every-independent-hold-is-released |
| reservation-data-rights-correction-accountability | include-minimum-coordinate-in-authorized-case-ledger-or-tenant-export | append-superseding-receipt | retain-minimum-accountability-receipt | retain-non-personal-minimum-accountability-receipt |
| reservation-processing-restriction-accountability | include-minimum-restriction-state-in-authorized-case-ledger-or-tenant-export | append-superseding-restriction-transition | enforce-until-every-active-case-is-released | retain-non-personal-minimum-restriction-proof |
| reservation-retention-control | include-in-controller-authorized-tenant-export | retry-through-the-same-execution-coordinate | block-destructive-mutation-while-any-independent-hold-is-active | dispose-execution-evidence-after-approved-audit-lifecycle |
| reservation-retention-proof-control | include-minimum-retention-proof-in-controller-authorized-tenant-export | immutable-owner-receipt-and-authority-bound-tombstone-only | exclude-terminal-record-from-ordinary-surfaces | retain-pii-free-local-proof-after-anonymisation |
